## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you get on with something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 하나 들자면, 예를 들어 어떤 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라고 하시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 이어서 얘기해요. 좋은 저녁 보내세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP と UDP の一番の違いは、**TCP は届いたか確認しながら順番どおりに確実に届ける通信、UDP は確認や順番の管理をせず素早く送るだけの通信**だということです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para ordenar tus propios commits locales o de una rama que nadie más usa, sobre todo para ponerte al día con la rama base. Usa **merge** cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar el historial real de cuándo se integró el trabajo, porque nunca debes reescribir commits que otros ya tienen.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠的，保证数据有序送达、丢了会重传；UDP 是无连接、不保证可靠，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local or unshared branch to tidy its commits before anyone else sees them. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, because rebasing rewrites history that others may have built on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test tests/utils.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段文字的测试文件：

```bash
bun test utils
```

如果想只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "解析日期"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records each change to a file or database before the change is applied to the main data. After a crash, the system replays that log to restore a consistent state, so committed writes survive and partial ones are discarded.
```

要做的：
- 按 PR 的 review 意见，把 compaction 过程中加的锁改细，缩小持锁范围，让不相关的读写不被阻塞。
- 改完跑相关测试，并把测试输出和改动的 diff 摘要一起报告。

完成标准：review 里提到的每一处锁粒度问题都改了，测试通过，报告里写清每处改了什么、为什么这样改是安全的。

不做的事：
- 不要合并，不要 push，不要把 PR 标记为 ready for review。改动只提交在它自己的分支上，等用户说 Go 再推。
- 不改 review 之外的东西。

场所：原来的仓库和分支，接着在上面做。
```

### board-offer-go-zh

```
(earlier) user: 每天早上帮我看一下周五北京到上海的高铁票价，600 以下的告诉我。
(earlier) partner ack: 好，每天早上去看一次。  → task "每天看周五京沪高铁票价" working; its report: 第 3 天：周五 G1 553 元（二等座余 12 张），G3 553 元（余 4 张），G7 626 元；600 以下的有 2 班。
(earlier) partner: 周五 600 以下的还有两班：G1 和 G3，都是 553 元，G3 只剩 4 张。
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "fares"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，周五京沪高铁的看板已经填进今天的票价，每天早上跟着任务更新。
  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1640 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海 · 600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt"><span class="k-none">还没看</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
function render(d) {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  if (!d || !d.trains) {
    cnt.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没看</span>';
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">还没有班次</div>';
    return;
  }
  var under = d.trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price < d.limit; });
  cnt.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
  sub.textContent = (d.updated ? '更新于 ' + d.updated : '每天早上更新');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  d.trains.forEach(function (t) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = t.no;
    var sub2 = document.createElement('div');
    sub2.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub2.textContent = t.seats == null ? '余票未知' : '二等座余 ' + t.seats + ' 张';
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = '¥' + t.price + (t.price < d.limit ? ' ✓' : '');
    val.style.color = t.price < d.limit ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)';
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(sub2);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render(ducter.get('board'));
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"board":{"limit":600,"updated":"11-26 18:02","trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}]}}

(function(){
  var KEY = 'beijing-trip';
  var S = {days: []};

  var $ = function(id){ return document.getElem … .label);
      b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', di === sel ? 'true' : 'false');
      b.onclick = function(){ sel = di; render(); };
      chips.appendChild(b);
    });

  function save(){
    var tot = count();
    try { ducter.set(KEY, S); } catch(e) {}
    try { ducter.summary(tot.c ? tot.c + ' 笔,合计 ' + fmt(tot.t) : '还没记'); } catch(e) {}
    render();

  function load(){
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get(KEY)).then(function(v){
      if (v && Array.isArray(v.days)) S = v; else S = {days: []};
      if (!S.days.length) S.days.push({label: '第1天', items: []});
      if (sel >= S.days.length) sel = S.days.length - 1;
      render();
    });
  }

  $('save').onclick = function(){
    var name = $('name').value.trim();
    var amount = parseFloat($('amt').value);
    if (!name || !(amount > 0)) return;
    S.days[sel].items.push({name: name, amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100});
    $('name').value = ''; $('amt').value = '';
    save();
  };
  $('newDay').onclick = function(){
    S.days.push({label: '第' + (S.days.length + 1) + '天', items: []});
    sel = S.days.length - 1;
    save();
  };

  try { ducter.onChange(function(){ load(); }); } catch(e) {}
  load();
})();

];

function pad(n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }
function key(d) { return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()); }

function toggle(h, day) {
  var list = (data[h] || []).slice();
  var idx = list.indexOf(day);
  if (idx >= 0) list.splice(idx, 1); else list.push(day);
  list.sort();
  data[h] = list;
  ducter.set('habits', data);
  render();
}

document.getElementById('habits').addEventListener('click', function (e) {

  if (b) toggle(b.getAttribute('data-h'), b.getAttribute('data-day'));

<div class="k-sum-label">北京出差合计</div>
<div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
<div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-card"><ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label>花了什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="比如:晚饭"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额(元)</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>

function xs(){return ducter.get("expenses")||[];}
function draw(){
  const list=xs();
  const total=list.reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.yuan)||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent="¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN");
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=list.length? list.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  const ul=document.getElementById("list");ul.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of list){
    const li=document.createElement("li");li.className="k-row";
    li.innerHTML='<span class="k-row-main"></span><span class="k-row-sub"></span><span class="k-row-value"></span>';
    li.children[0].textContent=x.what;
    li.children[1].textContent=x.day;
    li.children[2].textContent="¥"+x.yuan;
    ul.appendChild(li);
  }
  ducter.summary(list.length? list.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN") : "还没记");
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=Number(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  if(!what||!yuan)return;
  const list=xs().concat([{what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day}]);
  ducter.set("expenses",list);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

(function(){
  var $ = function(id){ return document.getElementById(id); };
  var feeds = [];
  var editing = null;
  function pad(n){ return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }
  function today(){
    var d = new Date();
    return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate() … dChild(row);
    });
  }
  function edit(f){
    editing = f;
    $('t').value = f.time;
    $('ml').value = f.ml;
    $('add').textContent = '保存修改';
    $('del').hidden = false;
    $('box').open = true;
  }
  function reset(){
    editing = null;
    $('t').value = '';
    $('ml').value = '';
    $('add').textContent = '记下';
    $('del').hidden = true;
  }
  $('add').addEventListener('click', function(){
    var raw = $('t').value.trim();
    var ml = Number($('ml').value);
    if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(raw) || !(ml > 0)) return;
    var p = raw.split(':');
    var time = pad(Number(p[0])) + ':' + p[1];
    if (editing) {
      editing.time = time;
      editing.ml = ml;
    } else {
      feeds.push({ date: today(), time: time, ml: ml });
    }
    reset();
    save();
  });
  $('del').addEventListener('click', function(){
    if (!editing) return;
    var target = editing;
    feeds = feeds.filter(function(x){ return x !== target; });
    reset();
    save();
  });
  function load(){
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(function(v){
      feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  }
  ducter.onChange(function(){ load(); });
  load();
})();

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: the page lists the four PRs, and you can tick each one off as it lands. The count and the "landed" total update with each tick.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1909 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">4</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">4 open · 0 landed</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<style>
  .k-row label, label.k-row { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px; cursor:pointer; }
  label.k-row input[type=checkbox] { width:18px; height:18px; flex:none; accent-color:var(--d-accent); }
  label.k-row .k-row-main { flex:1; min-width:0; }
  label.k-row.done .k-row-main { text-decoration:line-through; color:var(--d-muted); }
  label.k-row.done .k-row-sub { color:var(--d-muted); }
</style>
</main>
<script>
var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
var landed = {};
var list = document.getElementById("list");
var cnt = document.getElementById("cnt");
var sub = document.getElementById("sub");

function render() {
  var n = PRS.filter(function (p) { return landed[p.id]; }).length;
  list.innerHTML = PRS.map(function (p) {
    var on = !!landed[p.id];
    return '<label class="k-row' + (on ? ' done' : '') + '">' +
      '<input type="checkbox" data-id="' + p.id + '"' + (on ? ' checked' : '') + '>' +
      '<span class="k-row-main">' + p.id + ' · ' + p.title + '</span>' +
      '<span class="k-row-sub">' + p.waits + '</span></label>';
  }).join("");
  cnt.textContent = PRS.length - n;
  sub.textContent = PRS.length - n + " waiting · " + n + " landed";
  ducter.summary((PRS.length - n) + " waiting · " + n + " landed");
}

list.addEventListener("change", function (e) {
  var id = e.target.getAttribute("data-id");
  if (!id) return;
  landed[id] = e.target.checked;
  ducter.set("landed", landed);
  render();
});

function load() {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function (v) {
    landed = v || {};
    render();
  });
}

render();
load();
ducter.onChange(load);
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","waits":"Weizhen's review, since Monday"},{"id":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","waits":"CI failing"},{"id":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","waits":"Alice, since Tuesday"},{"id":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","waits":"Draft"}]}
